## T6. Decision

ATDR productization has a source-backed current-state lock, supervisor-template alignment, secure shell handoff, hardened read-only SOC Assistant behavior, Detection/ML contracts, SQLite-preserving persistence, durable background jobs, resumable ingestion, operational observability, PostgreSQL multi-worker coordination, deployment/recovery references, and controlled multi-source synthetic validation. GitHub Actions #50 validates the backend, frontend, and disposable PostgreSQL persistence/multi-worker workflows. The current local database was not reset or deleted. ATDR remains FastAPI + React + SQLAlchemy/Alembic, with SQLite for local development, optional PostgreSQL for shared deployment, local JWT/RBAC plus disabled-by-default MFU IAM, simulated analyst-approved response, and ML decision support only. The next evidence phases are provider-blinded/real-source Detection/ML validation and an approved-host rehearsal for shared storage, Linux services, TLS, monitoring, managed secrets, and measured recovery. Real-source hardware forwarding, production detection accuracy, completed provider-backed MFU IAM, real response enforcement, raw-log sharing with external LLMs, and production readiness remain unclaimed.

### v3.90 Decision Update

v3.90 completes the next product architecture slice: an opt-in durable queue and separately launched worker for allowlisted long operations. It keeps the FastAPI startup command unchanged, does not launch itself automatically, protects staged input paths, scopes job access by role/owner, fails evidence-mutating work closed on expired leases, and keeps response/model/IAM/LLM actions outside the dispatcher. The next reliability step is not more worker features on SQLite; it is managed-worker supervision plus PostgreSQL/multi-worker validation on an approved shared-lab environment. Production readiness is still not claimed.

### v3.92 Decision Update

v3.92 completes the local observability and single-worker supervision slice without adding an external service or schema migration. ATDR now distinguishes liveness from readiness, emits bounded metrics, rejects concurrent SQLite workers, records graceful worker stop state, surfaces operational warnings, and provides explicit protected audit retention. The next local productization step is resumable large-file ingestion and backpressure. PostgreSQL multi-worker, external monitoring/paging, and IAM preproduction validation remain separate environment-backed gates; production readiness is still not claimed.

### v3.93 Decision Update

v3.93 completes the local resumable-ingestion slice. Queued imports now commit evidence and checkpoint state atomically per chunk, renew worker ownership during long work, stop cooperatively at safe boundaries, and resume only from an unchanged staged input. Queue and storage pressure fail safely, cleanup is dry-run first, and the UI exposes committed progress without paths or fake ETA. The next phase is v3.94 PostgreSQL multi-worker runtime validation, managed worker deployment, shared staging design, and backup/restore concurrency drills on an approved host. Production readiness and global exactly-once ingestion are not claimed.

### v3.94 Decision Update

v3.94 completes the repository-side multi-worker contract: PostgreSQL skip-locked claim/recovery, private lease-generation fencing, shared storage ownership, concurrent source protection, graceful import handoff, backup coordination, isolated validators, ephemeral CI, and unprivileged systemd examples. GitHub Actions #49 passed the disposable PostgreSQL workflow, closing the v3.94 CI evidence item. SQLite remains the normal one-worker profile; multi-host mount permissions and managed-service behavior still require an approved host. No production readiness, global exactly-once, automatic response, real blocking, model promotion, or provider-backed IAM completion is claimed.

### v3.95 Decision Update

v3.95 completes the repository-side deployment-operations reference: optional HTTPS proxying with explicit trusted-forwarded-header boundaries, bounded Prometheus metrics/alerts, non-destructive scheduled reports, secret-management guidance, GET-only load testing, read-only backup verification, and isolated separate-target recovery validation. Local smoke/recovery and all GitHub Actions jobs passed without resetting or deleting the configured database. Approved-host installation, real certificate/monitoring/secret-provider operation, multi-host storage, and measured recovery objectives remain open; production readiness is not claimed.

### v3.96 Decision Update

v3.96 completes the strict repository and isolated-local rehearsal layer. The preflight refuses production profiles, broad proxy trust, placeholder secrets, unsafe staging/backups, stale backups, incomplete MFU handoff, non-simulated response, and raw-log assistant context. Pool/backup telemetry and alerts are bounded; the computed-case N+1 was reduced from 398 statements to 2, and the repeat GET-only load cleared the prior alert/case warnings. Separate-target recovery produced an honest synthetic measurement. Operational acceptance is blocked because the approved environment does not exist in the available context: the supervisor-named FQDN is unresolved and GitHub has no configured Environment. No remote deployment, IAM call, current-database mutation, secret exposure, model activation, response automation, or firewall call occurred; repository commit/push happened only after explicit approval.

### v3.97 Decision Update

v3.97 completes the local large-file reliability slice. The worker retains v3.93 transactional checkpoint/resume behavior while replacing row-by-row raw-text scans and flushes with indexed exact duplicate candidates and chunk-level persistence. A self-cleaning temporary validator passed 100,000 synthetic rows, graceful handoff, changed-input refusal, cooperative cancellation, bounded memory, and zero detection/label/model/response side effects while proving the configured database unchanged. The additive fingerprint migration was validated only on disposable databases and remains unapplied to the current DB until the operator explicitly upgrades. SQLite remains one-worker local storage; sustained PostgreSQL/shared-storage capacity, real-device traffic, and an SLA remain unclaimed.

### v3.98 Decision Update

v3.98 completes the repository-side frozen holdout evaluator and records a conservative current-corpus result. Exact/near/feature duplicate components remain within one partition, fit/calibration/threshold/final roles stay separate, and database/model/response state remains unchanged. Three random diagnostics show strong queue recall and F1 but unstable FPR; source and temporal validation cannot pass with the current provenance/support. Readiness remains `candidate_only`, external independence is not claimed, and no active artifact or response behavior changes.

### v3.99 Decision Update

v3.99 closes the missing controlled source/time separation layer with a reproducible 720-row evidence pack spanning three synthetic source identities and four collection windows. Exact raw, normalized near-pattern, and used-feature fingerprints are quarantined against the reviewed corpus before scoring; the supervised candidate, calibration, and threshold remain frozen from internal roles, and external rows contribute nothing to fitting or selection. Controlled source/temporal/grouped metrics are stable with zero benign-like false positives and full suspicious/malicious recall, but calibration fails every split and the corpus remains scenario-authored synthetic evidence. These results validate pipeline behavior, not real-world accuracy. Readiness remains `candidate_only`; no model, label, response action, firewall behavior, configured database, or active artifact changed.

### v4.0 Decision Update

v4.0 closes the missing public-provider evidence step with verified CSE-CIC-IDS2018 files, label-independent min-hash sampling, self-hashed manifests, direct-only field mapping, dual-reference overlap quarantine, and prediction-before-label ordering. The protocol passed, but the frozen candidate did not generalize: every benign flow was queued and calibration was weak. This benchmark is now locked final evidence and must not be used to tune thresholds, guards, features, or models. Readiness remains `candidate_only`; no operational database, label, active artifact, detection run, response action, or firewall state changed.

### v4.1 Decision Update

v4.1 establishes explicit Palo Alto, generic syslog, provider-flow, and raw-fallback contracts with availability-aware common features and no fabricated firewall fields. Its verified separate CSE-CIC-IDS2018 development corpus was never imported into ATDR labels, and the v4.0 provider benchmark remained hash-locked before and after the complete diagnostic. Random-split pooled and schema-routed results are useful development signals, but weak calibration, provider source/time instability, and failed schema-held-out transfer prevent model activation or promotion. Readiness remains `candidate_only`; automatic response and real firewall blocking remain disabled. The next ML gate is a separately governed untouched benchmark and authorized, independently collected multi-source real firewall/syslog evidence.

### v4.2 Decision Update

v4.2 makes the read-only SOC Assistant presentation-ready without changing detection, ML, IAM, response, schema, or startup behavior. Answers expose citation-derived ATDR grounding, default sections are concise, provider labeling distinguishes configured from actually used Gemini, and only redacted whitelisted session state survives route navigation. The MFU burgundy/gold shell remains React-native and uses the official supervisor template as visual reference only. Gemini and the deterministic fallback remain explanation layers over ATDR evidence, never detectors or actors. Full verification passed. The configured database was not migrated under this goal; its inherited v3.97 additive migration remains an explicit operator step before normal dashboard use.

### v4.3 Decision Update

v4.3 completes the repository and local-runtime portability scope for mandatory MFU shell entry. A teammate can provide the separately approved shell path once, then use Windows-safe setup/start/check/stop commands from arbitrary project locations; private paths, environment files, secrets, launcher state, logs, and backups remain ignored. Shell mode rejects direct local login, while recovery remains explicit. A disposable path-with-spaces simulation brought all four services ready and preserved the configured database and environment hashes. Real provider-backed MFU account acceptance, approved Google client configuration, project group assignment, 2FA lifecycle, and deprovisioning remain external gates. No production-readiness, model-promotion, automatic-response, or real-firewall claim is made.

### v4.4 Decision Update

v4.4 completes everything locally controllable for MFU authentication stabilization. The approved shell no longer falls back to a legacy Google client; setup/start/check require matching private frontend/backend client configuration and expose only status booleans and diagnosis codes. The composed Express handoff route, one-time exchange, domain/role mapping, safe errors, explicit recovery, and lifecycle cleanup are covered by focused, full, frontend, external-shell, and release-gate evidence. Startup intentionally refuses to launch the shell while the approved OAuth client is absent. Real MFU account acceptance remains an external provider gate and is not claimed. No database reset, detection/ML change, model activation, response automation, real firewall action, or secret disclosure occurred.

### v4.5 Decision Update

v4.5 completes the locally controllable reproducibility and executive SOC baseline. A disposable clean-room Windows setup reached Alembic head from no environment or private state; installation and provider readiness are separate; the external shell has a versioned structural contract; Overview and AI Governance use one canonical evidence snapshot without historical fallback; model states are unambiguous; the five primary SOC pages share a responsive summary-first header; the assistant is bounded, persistent, grounded, mutation-free, and rendered through a reusable answer/citation component; core pages pass rendered projector/laptop/mobile overflow checks; and personal paths were removed from the full tracked tree. Backend, frontend, replay, performance, and release gates pass. The exact 175-path boundary was approved and published as `dd6ff01`; CI-only corrections remained confined to `.github/workflows/ci.yml`; GitHub Actions CI #55 passed all jobs at `1535a31`; and a final public clone was clean and passed focused verification. The current DB and protected material were not reset, deleted, staged, or published. Shell distribution, university OAuth acceptance, approved-host operations, and independent real-source ML evidence remain external product gates. Production readiness is not claimed.

### v4.6 Decision Update

v4.6 closes the locally controllable MFU shell distribution gap. The reviewed shell is represented by a deterministic sanitized companion archive with a contract-locked checksum, source fingerprint, manifest, required-file list, and explicit private/generated exclusions. Setup verifies and installs a versioned release under ignored runtime storage, start/check validate its integrity, and a clean Windows clone with spaces completed all dependency installation and migrations on a new disposable SQLite database. Installation readiness is true while provider readiness remains independently false; startup fails closed with one concise blocker until authorized private OAuth/IAM configuration is supplied. No configured database, credentials, model, response action, firewall behavior, or protected evidence changed. Production readiness and live MFU provider acceptance are not claimed.

### v4.7 Decision Update

v4.7 closes the locally controllable large-SQLite Overview query-shape gap. Read-only timing and query plans identified one wide normalized-log scan, ten recent-alert evidence queries, and five cache-signature round trips. Existing indexes now serve quality counts, alert evidence is correlated, and freshness checks use one stronger portable statement. Five-run cold application-cache and warm targets pass with exact response semantics, write invalidation, concurrent SQLite reads, PostgreSQL compilation, and no ML/response side effects. No migration, index, configured-database write, TTL increase, prewarming, frontend change, model action, response automation, or real blocking occurred. True OS-cold and approved-host PostgreSQL capacity remain monitored environment evidence; production readiness is not claimed.

### v4.8 Decision Update

v4.8 closes the locally controllable integrated-acceptance gap. One fail-closed CLI now applies existing migrations to a disposable SQLite database and composes real source, queue, worker, resumable ingestion, parser, detection, alert, case, explanation, assistant, metrics, dashboard, and persistence services. Nine focused tests and a 50,000-log run passed exact counts, interruption/cancel resume, stale-lease failure, source-scoped port-scan deduplication, investigation traceability, assistant context/citations/redaction/provider-failure fallback, backup/restore, configured-DB preservation, and temp cleanup. It created no users, labels, model runs, or response actions. External provider, approved-host PostgreSQL, real-device, and independent real-traffic evidence remain open; production readiness is not claimed.

### v4.9 Decision Update

v4.9 closes the locally controllable Detection/ML reliability-contract gap. ATDR now has a versioned evidence-bounded taxonomy and rule catalog, source-scoped five-minute correlation, validated Palo Alto field anchoring, causal source-scoped features, leakage/duplicate quarantine, isolated fit/calibration/threshold/final roles, five required split views, locked external evidence, honest explanations, and unambiguous active-versus-diagnostic registry states. The 24-scenario controlled corpus, assistant safety regression, backend/frontend suites, performance smoke, and release gate pass. No supervised or hybrid candidate passes every strict split and external gate, so readiness remains `candidate_only`; no label, model artifact, activation, response action, automatic response, or real firewall change occurred. Independent multi-device/time evidence and a new untouched schema-compatible benchmark remain required before any promotion discussion.

### v5.1 Decision Update

v5.1 closes the locally controllable governed-activation gap without weakening v4.9. A fresh calibrated ExtraTrees binary SOC queue is reproducibly registered with dataset/feature/provenance/calibration/threshold/checksum/code metadata and active in `shadow_observation`. It passes artifact, leakage, serialization, probability, and latency safety checks, but strict reliability passes 0/5 and the locked external gate remains failed. Therefore model output is evidence-only: rules remain alert-authoritative, decision-support influence fails closed, production promotion is rejected, and response automation/real blocking remain false. Disposable private-file validation proves parsing and shadow scoring on current PAN-OS structure without changing configured state, but it has no labels and is not accuracy evidence. Independent reviewed multi-device/time and untouched schema-compatible evidence remain required before any stronger lifecycle state.

### v5.2 Decision Update

v5.2 closes the reproducible controlled-layer repair and shadow-reliability instrumentation scope. Timestamp-cadence, field-poor anomaly interpretation, advisory threshold authority, and primary-rule precedence repairs move the layered matrix from 267/288 to 288/288 with zero controlled FP/FN and zero responses. The supervised evaluator compares calibrated/weighted strategies across temporal, source, zone-proxy, and random views, but no candidate passes every required gate; source holdout fails closed and the locked external benchmark remains failed. The leading comparator is not selected. Aggregate telemetry stores no raw/private evidence, AI Governance and alert explanations distinguish authority layers, and the full release matrix passes. Lifecycle remains `shadow_observation`; production promotion, response automation, and real blocking remain false.

### v5.3 Decision Update

v5.3 freezes v5.2 state and identifies the temporal failure as a chronological
target/provenance/application-context shift rather than primarily parser-schema
OOD. Three disjoint rolling future windows reproduce near-total false-positive
queueing. Fit-only OOD and honest abstention expose uncertainty but do not hide
queue cost or pass the fixed gates. Calibrated HistGradientBoosting is only the
best aggregate diagnostic comparator and remains unselected. Source holdout
fails closed, locked external evidence remains failed and untouched, all
database/artifact/response counts remain unchanged, rules stay authoritative,
and lifecycle remains `shadow_observation`.

v5.4 records immutable aggregate fingerprints for every v5.3 evidence role,
rolling window, locked external result, and governed artifact. It creates a
development-only manifest with 1,467 rows and excludes 532 locked final plus
236 duplicate-quarantine rows with zero overlap. Chronological audit confirms
material application, provenance, schema, label, and review-density shift.
Aggregate shadow monitoring reports `OOD Warning`. A private 773,551-row
preflight supplies eight operational windows but only one device and no ground
truth. The optional 200-row pack remains assisted/weak and non-import-ready.
No label, model, detection run, response, or artifact changed; lifecycle stays

### v5.5 Decision Update

v5.5 validates the v5.4 lock and confines fitting, calibration, threshold
selection, and strategy ranking to 1,467 development rows. Five memory-only
strategies run across three leakage-safe chronological folds. The three-class
ExtraTrees SOC queue is frozen as the diagnostic leader before one read-only
locked regression, but it passes zero strict development folds. Locked FPR
improves from v5.3's `0.9976` to `0.0773`, while F1 `0.4925`, suspicious
recall `0.3824`, malicious recall `0.4143`, and ECE `0.5405` remain below the

Database and artifact state remain unchanged; no labels, model runs,
detection runs, responses, activations, promotions, or authoritative alert

### v5.6 Decision Update

v5.6 streams all 773,551 private PAN-OS rows through bounded disposable
storage, parses every row, quarantines 120,626 rows including 120,000
configured-DB overlaps, and keeps all exact/near families inside one
chronological role. It applies a fixed conservative assisted policy with zero
human-reviewed private labels, excludes 131,180 ambiguous development events,
and gives assisted samples lower weight than governed human evidence.

Calibrated HistGradientBoosting is frozen before future-label access. Its
3,400-row private future result has F1 `0.9889`, FPR `0.0211`, and

accuracy. IsolationForest contamination `0.02` reduces private future FPR to
`0.0057` but captures only `0.4576` of queue evidence and `0.16` of suspicious
evidence. Configured database and active artifacts remain unchanged; no label,
model run, detection run, alert, response, activation, promotion, or
authoritative alert change occurs. Lifecycle stays `shadow_observation` and
rules remain alert-authoritative.

### v5.7 Decision Update

v5.7 audits and preserves every v5.3-v5.6 evidence role, records a current
ignored lock audit, and freezes the v5.6 calibrated HistGradientBoosting
candidate with its 40-field feature contract, preprocessing, sigmoid
calibration, threshold `0.3`, training-manifest, code, and artifact identities.
The candidate is threshold-only and has no post-prediction suppression guard.

The complete private preflight parses 773,551/773,551 rows, finds 120,000

PAN-OS-compatible independently labeled corpus. The evaluator therefore
returns `independent_evidence_required`, reveals no labels, reports no blind

Prediction mode now creates an immutable, fingerprinted freeze and a
prediction-blind review pack. Reveal requires an unchanged evidence contract,
complete allowed human/provider provenance, and advisor approval; the first
successful reveal is sealed and cannot be repeated. Configured DB and model

or responses are created. Lifecycle remains `shadow_observation`, rules remain

disabled.

### v5.8 Decision Update

v5.8 validates the exact frozen candidate/artifact/code/feature/calibration
contract and provides a disabled-by-default, bounded, read-only normalized-log
shadow runtime. A measured 100-row run exposes aggregate queue, confidence,
drift, rule disagreement, and persisted anomaly telemetry while changing no
database or artifact state. Reused evidence fails intake and no unlabeled

### v5.9 Decision Update

v5.9 adds disabled-by-default, append-only aggregate observations with
source/time/row bounds, deterministic idempotency, admin-only durable
execution, cooperative cancellation, explicit audited retention, authenticated
summary APIs, and a compact AI Governance trend. Row-level evidence, labels,

The complete private development file was inspected in disposable storage:
all 773,551 rows parsed across eight chronological windows, maximum
application shift was `0.117569`, maximum schema shift was `0.001429`, and

independent holdout, so no accuracy claim is made. Official-source review

### v5.10 Decision Update

v5.10 plans four opaque configured-database source scopes and eight bounded,
non-overlapping chronological scopes. All eight observations complete and
pass the eight operational gates; a repeated execution creates zero rows and
reuses all eight idempotency keys. Aggregate results expose material queue and
rule/shadow disagreement variation, three OOD-warning scopes, and two
insufficient-evidence scopes rather than hiding them.

No label is read and no accuracy metric is calculated. Outputs exclude source
identity, raw logs, IPs, paths, fingerprints, and secrets. No alert, case,
label, model run, detection run, response action, activation, or promotion
changes. The inherited cold AI Governance bottleneck is repaired through a
covering index and query-plan/shared-aggregate changes, reducing measured
cold profiling from roughly ten seconds to about 0.3 seconds with equivalent
responses. Lifecycle remains `shadow_observation`; rules remain authoritative;
independent multi-device labeled evidence remains the advancement gate.

### v5.11 Decision Update

v5.11 explains the v5.10 warnings using aggregate operational evidence only.
Application-distribution shift is the dominant cause, parser limitations
explain three observations, and two scopes are too sparse to support a stable
conclusion. Candidate score/queue movement occurs twice, rule/shadow
disagreement movement once, and IsolationForest does not cross its variation
threshold. None of these observations establishes prediction correctness.

Fixed thresholds and conservative hysteresis now prevent single-observation
state flapping without changing the model or prediction threshold. A bounded
durable monitoring job is present but disabled by default and has no
always-on scheduler. AI Governance exposes opaque aggregate diagnostics only.
Disposable retention rehearsal preserves every authoritative entity and does
not access the configured database. Lifecycle remains `shadow_observation`;
rules remain authoritative; independent multi-device labeled evidence remains
the advancement gate.

### v5.13.1 Decision Update

The cumulative v4.9-v5.13 worktree is now one reviewable 181-path boundary.
Every path is classified and present exactly once in the master allowlist.
The three paths omitted from historical phase allowlists are retained because
runtime imports, tests, or startup guidance prove they belong to the program.
No duplicate, orphaned, oversized, private, generated-report, or evidence-backed
deletion candidate was found.

The complete local verification matrix passes. No file is staged, no commit or
push is authorized, and no runtime, database, label, model, rule-authority,
response, or historical-evidence behavior is changed by this closure. The next
technical phase should remain gated by independent multi-device evidence and
real-device/parser-profile validation rather than stronger production claims.

### v5.14 Decision Update

ATDR now has measured privacy-safe evidence that its real resumable ingestion
and deterministic detection stack can process 100,000 rows from a large
private PAN-OS stream in disposable SQLite storage. Bounded commits,
interruption and resume, cancellation, idempotent enqueue, lock waiting,
source counters and health, alert/case traceability, and dashboard queries all
passed. The configured database was unchanged, no private path or row evidence
was returned, and no response, label, model-run, activation, or promotion
occurred.

This is runtime acceptance, not accuracy or production evidence. Only one real
device and collection period were observed; the two logical source windows are
explicitly simulated; no independent ground-truth labels were available; and
approved-host PostgreSQL/live-syslog capacity remains external. Rules remain
alert-authoritative, supervised ML remains `shadow_observation`, and response
automation and real firewall blocking remain disabled.

### v5.15 Decision Update

ATDR now has measured local evidence that its current staging, durable job,
transactional ingestion, source-quality, deterministic detection,
investigation, and dashboard services can process the complete 773,551-row
private PAN-OS stream through progressive 250,000, 500,000, and full-file
checkpoints. Exact counters, database integrity, repeated handoff,
cancellation/resume, stale-lease fail-closed recovery, idempotency, lock
waiting, privacy, configured-database preservation, and cleanup passed.

The run peaked at about 12 GiB traced Python memory, so it is not a production
capacity claim. One device, one time range, absent independent labels, local
SQLite, and simulated logical windows still block multi-device, accuracy, and
deployment conclusions. Rules remain alert-authoritative, supervised ML
remains `shadow_observation`, and response automation and real firewall
blocking remain disabled.
